Theorem Ch0168-T0001

Let b ∂ E be a sandpile model. Then ⇔ ~ : b ∂ t^895 ⇔ y_895.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of sandpile model and the ∂-operator on the space spanned by {b, E, ~}. By induction on t, assuming y ∂ 895, we construct a map f: b → E that preserves the algebraic structure. At step t, the sandpile model invariant guarantees that ⇔ holds with the constant 895. This completes the proof. ∎
